{"lang_code":"en","pos":"name","senses":[{"glosses":["A rule of thumb stating that a chemical compound is likely to be an orally active drug in humans unless it violates more than one of these criteria: (i) no more than 5 hydrogen bond donors (the total number of nitrogen–hydrogen and oxygen–hydrogen bonds); (ii) no more than 10 hydrogen bond acceptors (all nitrogen or oxygen atoms); (iii) a molecular mass less than 500 daltons; (iv) an octanol-water partition coefficient (log P) that does not exceed 5."],"synonyms":[{"word":"Pfizer's rule of five"},{"word":"RO5"},{"word":"rule of five"}],"topics":["chemistry","natural-sciences","physical-sciences"]}],"upstream_line":7814996,"upstream_offset":17996127629,"upstream_record_sha256":"bd1e8849ed55b5e9b8f25a02c308a36fb0b7465421ce6d8816ffb30104a62a87","word":"Lipinski's rule of five"}

{"forms":[{"form":"layups","tags":["plural"]},{"form":"lay-up","tags":["alternative"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["A close-range shot in which the shooter banks the ball off the backboard from a few feet away."],"topics":["ball-games","basketball","games","hobbies","lifestyle","sports"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Meeting the numbers will be a layup, if not a slam dunk.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A relatively easy task."],"tags":["colloquial"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The ship endured an interminable layup in the harbor lasting nearly a month.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The state of being laid up."]},{"examples":[{"text":"The caboose, long decrepit, rested on a forgotten layup just north of the dry riverbed.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A track used to store train cars."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"],"topics":["rail-transport","railways","transport"]},{"glosses":["A track used to store train cars.","A side track, tunnel, or elevated structure where idle trains park during off-peak hours, overnight, or on weekend;
